Mac run 37219249802 for ab30587acb0f0eecc2dc872aff57ba1cf04814fc passed both the complete scripted app checks with view snapshots and the Xcode application build. This accepts the selected-manifest verification-proof binding, including refusal to reuse an unchanged file proof across a changed manifest. Engine CI 37217132776 for ad16c67974571b4b4cec51d6b3c8e139777c2540 and 37220007613 for c908c961134ac2e26092f9d5bdd80db7640faa8e each passed weights-free, coverage and external public-library jobs. They cover the long-conversation preparation and standalone config/index changes respectively, with their dependencies. Complete metadata and raw logs are retained below. These remote jobs load no actual model and do not establish outcome quality or measured performance.

Review of the actual image path found that deployed query-tiled vision attention already bounds vision workspace independently of the candidate's language prefill cap. A 512-row language cap alone is not evidence that full photographs cannot fit. The later native source now requires and records query tile 256 with no attention padding for image sessions. A metadata-only native regression checks both original and candidate plans at the declared photograph budget, preserving streamed drafts and the complete context while pricing both photograph dimensions, generated fixtures and the maximum image grid. It uses the real planner, resize geometry, source pixel charge and vision workspace formula; it does not load weights or execute the tower. These newly added native assertions have not compiled or run at capture time.

The Python image-session validator rejects missing, changed or wrongly typed image arithmetic identity. All twelve driver groups pass on both Python runtimes. Actual image capacity and answers still require the separately frozen physical run. The original general-task campaign's frozen binary and helpers remain unchanged.

Raw logs may contain terminal control codes; they are preserved as data. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>, with both original and normalized hashes and byte counts. Every compressed record is roundtrip checked.
